========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Hi, Doctor. It's really nice to meet you. Thank you so much for taking the time to see me. 

Um, well, I guess I'm here because... gosh, I hope I explain this right, I really don't want to sound, you know, completely unreasonable or anything. But I work as a mechanic, and lately I've been... well, I keep hearing this man's voice. It comes right out of the garage vents at the shop, and sometimes even from under the hood when I'm working on a vehicle. It's just as clear as somebody standing right next to me. 

He keeps telling me... it's actually a little embarrassing to say out loud... but he tells me not to trust my husband. Which is awful, because my husband is wonderful, really. And the voice also tells me not to touch certain cars at work. 

I just... I really hope that doesn't sound too strange? I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right information and doing this okay.

Um... well, it started a little while ago when we upgraded some of the tech at the shop. I just started noticing how the new EV chargers were hooked into the main network, and... gosh, I feel so silly saying this out loud to you. I really don't want to sound like a bad wife or a problem employee. 

But... I know it sounds completely far-fetched, I really do, but I’m still convinced my husband and the shop owner have been using the EV chargers and the security cameras to track exactly what I’m doing. It's like they're monitoring my bays.

I smiled and agreed with them because I didn't want to cause a fight or seem ungrateful for their help. But... inside? My feelings haven't changed. Even when they show me the footage and say there’s nothing there, I just... I still know it's happening. 

I'm so sorry, I know that must sound so stubborn of me. Am I giving you the right kind of details? I just want to make sure I'm answering your questions properly and being as helpful as I can.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I... yeah. I know it sounds so far-fetched, and I really, really hate being so stubborn about it. I know my husband is wonderful, and I know he wouldn't want to hurt me. And I know they showed me the footage, and I know what it looked like on the screen. 

But... yes. I'm still convinced they've been using the EV chargers and the security cameras to track what I'm doing. It's like... even when they say there's nothing there, and even when they show me, I just... I can't let it go. It feels completely unshakable. 

I'm so sorry, I know that's not what you want to hear, and I know it makes me sound like a terrible wife and a difficult person. I promise I'm usually very reasonable. I just...
